- Kind: page; layout: layouts/application.html.erb (PersonController declares no layout); audited: yes
- Person overview. _hero (hero_panel, back slot = Switch to HQ Interface) > _banners (ui_callout_box x up to 4) > _summary_strip (ui_section action:_actions > _photo + 4 ui_label_value groups under raw h3) > _teams_positions (ui_section > ui_accordion x2) > _permissions (ui_section > ui_callout_box > ui_accordion > ui_table) > _contact_details (ui_section > ui_label_value); also common/_photo_none
- Partials reached: `common/_photo_none.html.erb`, `person/_banners.html.erb`, `person/_contact_details.html.erb`, `person/_hero.html.erb`, `person/_permissions.html.erb`, `person/_photo.html.erb`, `person/_summary_group_heading.html.erb`, `person/_summary_strip.html.erb`, `person/_teams_positions.html.erb`, `person/_timestamp_value.html.erb`, `person/_actions.html.erb`, `person/_teams_positions_actions.html.erb`
